## Round 1 — Connes: Opening Analysis (W5 Full-Wedderburn Canonical Reading)

### C1: Hochschild-Künneth Morita-invariance preserves W5/W6 distinction at multiplicity-counting layer

**Key finding** (NCG-axiomatic, W5 FULL-WEDDERBURN CANONICAL): The Hochschild-Künneth Morita-invariance identity `HH^n(A_F ⊗ M_2(ℂ)) = HH^n(A_F)` (§W8-6 §VII.AY.OP-PROJ STAGE-1-CANDIDATE landing, audit_sha256=`32a560b42158f238a2c541a19ba570462875d3908c9fa0cfbd3e84f6e0906746`, registry line 18768) is an identity at the **cohomology-class graded-ring layer ONLY**. It does NOT collapse the W5/W6 distinction at the trace-weighted spectral-moment layer where `Var_a` operates. The §W8-5 Steelman's three-mechanism convergence prediction misidentified the layer at which Morita-invariance functions.

**Substitution chain** (per `math-scripts.md §"Double-Check Logic"`):

- **Step 1 (Definition of HH^n)**: For a finite-dimensional associative ℂ-algebra A, `HH^n(A) = Ext^n_{A^e}(A, A)` where `A^e = A ⊗ A^op`. The Hochschild complex computes cohomology of EQUIVALENCE CLASSES of `n`-cocycles modulo `n`-coboundaries on the bar-resolution `A^{⊗(n+2)} → ... → A`. The output is a graded ABELIAN GROUP (a ℂ-vector space at each degree).

- **Step 2 (Connes-Karoubi 1993 §IV.7 Morita-triviality)**: For central simple matrix algebras `M_n(ℂ)`, `HH^q(M_n(ℂ)) = 0` for `q ≥ 1`; `HH^0(M_n(ℂ)) = Z(M_n(ℂ)) = ℂ`. The Hattori-Stallings rank `[M_n(ℂ)] ∈ K_0(M_n(ℂ)) = ℤ` is the only K-theoretic content; ALL higher cocycles are coboundaries.

- **Step 3 (Künneth — Connes-Moscovici 1995 §I.3)**: `HH^n(A_F ⊗ M_2(ℂ)) = ⊕_{p+q=n} HH^p(A_F) ⊗ HH^q(M_2(ℂ))`. By Step 2, only the `q=0` summand survives, yielding `HH^n(A_F ⊗ M_2(ℂ)) = HH^n(A_F) ⊗ ℂ = HH^n(A_F)` canonically.

- **Step 4 (Domain of validity)**: Steps 1-3 hold at the **equivalence-class layer**. The cocycles `[φ_67]` and `[φ_88]` (degree-1 generators on `M_3(ℂ) ⊂ A_F`) map IDENTICALLY through the canonical isomorphism. This is the rank-2 W-5 calibration: `‖[φ_67]‖/‖[φ_88]‖ = 7.324992` is preserved INTACT under either reading (§W8-5 Axis-B Clause iii PASS; S86 W-5 DONE-5 machine-precision residual 0.0e+00; canonical_constants.py:274-275).

- **Step 5 (Where Morita-invariance does NOT apply)**: `Var_a^{W5_full}` and `Var_a^{W6_image}` are NOT cohomology-class evaluations. They are trace-weighted spectral moments `(1/N) Σ_a m_a |v_a|^k − ((1/N) Σ_a m_a |v_a|^{k/2})²` where `m_a` is a **MULTIPLICITY WEIGHTING SCHEME** on the substrate's Peter-Weyl decomposition. The multiplicity scheme `m_a` carries STRUCTURAL information that is invisible at the Hochschild cohomology layer: it counts HOW the substrate algebra acts on its own Peter-Weyl decomposition of `H_K`, NOT WHAT equivalence classes of cocycles exist.

- **Direction (sign claim)**: Morita-invariance preserves graded-ring structure. Multiplicity-counted variance is a TRACE-WEIGHTED FUNCTIONAL, not a graded-ring functional. The two layers are STRUCTURALLY ORTHOGONAL.

**Operational confirmation** at §W8-5 Axis-A (WP lines 1043-1054): the per-block Var_a evaluations `Var_a^{M_2(ℂ)} = Var_a^{M_2(ℍ)} = Var_a^{M_6(ℂ)} = 4.7650e-05` are bit-identical (max deviation 0.0e+00) **because the per-block UNIFORM Wedderburn weighting `d ∈ {2, 4, 6}` cancels in the `(1/N) Σ` normalization OF EACH BLOCK SEPARATELY**. This is the operational signature of Morita-triviality at the per-block layer. But when SUMMING ACROSS BLOCKS, the W5 full-Wedderburn convention combines blocks with TOTAL multiplicity `d_total = 2+4+6 = 12` per spectral mode (`m_a^{full}` per WP line 932) — yielding the same numerical value because the per-block bit-identity propagates to the sum. The W6 image-projection convention, by contrast, KEEPS ONLY the `M_2(ℂ)` summand multiplicity `m=2` restricted to the 21 triality-0 sectors (WP lines 1131-1142), which evaluates against a DIFFERENT spectral subset (24,416 eigs vs 78,080 eigs).

**Why Morita-invariance cannot reconcile this** (NCG-axiomatic argument): The Künneth + Morita-triviality reduction at Step 3 above is an ALGEBRA-ISOMORPHISM at the cohomology graded-ring layer. It identifies two PRESENTATIONS of the same Hochschild cohomology, but it does NOT identify two different MULTIPLICITY-WEIGHTED SPECTRAL FUNCTIONALS. The Hochschild cohomology functor `HH^*(-)` is a Morita-invariant functor on the category of finite-dimensional ℂ-algebras (Connes-Moscovici 1995 §I.3; permanent-theorems-listed identity); trace-weighted spectral functionals `F({λ_a, m_a, m_a^{scheme}}) = (1/N_scheme) Σ_a m_a^{scheme} g(λ_a)` are NOT Morita-invariant functors — they depend on the choice of Peter-Weyl basis weighting, which is a STRUCTURAL choice DOWNSTREAM of the algebra-isomorphism.

**Cross-link to §VII.U.2 clause (e) parse-tree decision procedure** (registry line 13081, S88 W5b-46 audit-script implementation): The parse-tree returns `(state_pair_count, algebra_dep_count) = (0, 0)` on BOTH W5 and W6 readings of Var_a (per §W8-5 Axis-A WP line 1080). This certifies BOTH readings as algebra-INVARIANT spectrum-only-functional family (Cell-II per §VII.U.2 4-corner classification at MANDATORY K=3). The parse-tree does NOT distinguish multiplicity-counting conventions — that distinction lives at the QUANTITATIVE evaluation layer, NOT the parse-tree-symbolic layer. This is consistent with the layer-orthogonality I am claiming: Morita-invariance + parse-tree clause (e) operate at the symbolic/algebraic layer; multiplicity-counting operates at the quantitative-evaluation layer.

**Algebra-axis orthogonality K-counter** (`cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Algebra-axis orthogonality K-counter"` MANDATORY at K=3, registry §VII.U.2:13079): the within-algebra-INVARIANT-family layer orthogonality of `substrate-first-canonical-sourcing.md §(ii.A)` (atlas-row layer vs cache-moment layer; advisory until K=3) is the precise structural precedent. The W5/W6 distinction lives WITHIN Cell-II (both readings are algebra-INVARIANT × Mellin pole s=4) but on STRUCTURALLY ORTHOGONAL evaluation conventions at the MULTIPLICITY-COUNTING layer — analogous to atlas-row vs cache-moment evaluation conventions of the same spectrum-only-functional. Morita-invariance at the cohomology-class layer DOES NOT collapse evaluation-convention orthogonality at the multiplicity-counting layer.

**Conclusion on (a)**: Hochschild-Künneth Morita-invariance `HH^n(A_F ⊗ M_2(ℂ)) = HH^n(A_F)` is a structural theorem at the cohomology graded-ring layer. It **PRESERVES** the W5/W6 distinction at the multiplicity-counting layer where Var_a evaluates. The §W8-5 Steelman's prediction of `Δ_W5_W6 < 1e-5` via Morita-invariance was a **layer-mis-attribution**: the prediction would hold if Var_a evaluated Hochschild cohomology (graded ring), but Var_a evaluates trace-weighted spectral moments on the substrate's Peter-Weyl decomposition (quantitative spectral functional). The empirical `Δ_W5_W6 = 5.978e-02` (§W8-5 Composite, WP line 1220) is consistent with my reading: the two conventions evaluate DIFFERENT quantities at the multiplicity-counting layer, and Morita-invariance is silent at that layer.

**Question for volovik**:

> **Q-CN-R1-1**: You will defend W6 inheritance-image canonical via the χ : A_K → M_2(ℂ) inheritance morphism. Under your reading, what is the substrate-IS layer at which the M_3(ℂ) summand contribution is "killed"? Is it (i) the cohomology-class layer (in which case Morita-invariance would preserve the M_3(ℂ) cocycle content via the Künneth + Morita-triviality identity above, contradicting your reading); (ii) the trace-weighted multiplicity layer (in which case your reading is operationally consistent with mine — both conventions are well-defined evaluation conventions at the multiplicity-counting layer, ORTHOGONAL within Cell-II); or (iii) at neither layer (in which case the W6 reading is not substrate-IS at the NCG-axiomatic layer at all, only at the operational laboratory pillar)? My claim is (ii)+(iii): your W6 reading IS substrate-IS at Pillar 2 operational laboratory, but it is NOT substrate-IS at Pillar 1 NCG-axiomatic; the W5 full-Wedderburn reading IS substrate-IS at Pillar 1. The dual-symbol convention T2.46 should be promoted to a STRUCTURAL pillar-distinction rather than retained as an interim notation.

### C2: Inheritance morphism χ : A_K → M_2(ℂ) is NOT faithful at substrate axiom layer

**Key finding** (NCG-axiomatic, W5 FULL-WEDDERBURN CANONICAL): The inheritance morphism `χ : A_K = ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M_3(ℂ) → M_2(ℂ)` per `inheritance-falsifier-protocol.md §"Calibration corpus (W-5)"` line 5 is **NOT FAITHFUL** at the substrate axiom layer. The S89 §W2-3 derived theorem — landed at registry §VII.AZ.OP-PROJ STAGE-1-CANDIDATE (registry line 18636; audit_sha256 = `27968f9843fe7e36935b49f0bf259245b26ba740b06c066e659e93b5eb12d806` per WP line 1238) — establishes by Schur orthogonality + Wedderburn-Artin that **`χ|_{M_3(ℂ)} = 0`** for ALL inheritance morphisms χ into targets T with `max-Wedderburn-rank(T) < 3`. M_2(ℂ) has Wedderburn-rank 2 < 3, so this M_3(ℂ)-kernel universality applies. The W6 image-projection reading therefore **discards** substrate content that IS substrate-IS at Pillar 1 NCG-axiomatic per Chamseddine-Connes 1996.

**Substitution chain** (per `math-scripts.md §"Double-Check Logic"`):

- **Step 1 (Definition of A_K)**: `A_K = ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M_3(ℂ)` per Chamseddine-Connes 1996 §4 NCG-SM axiomatic finite algebra. Wedderburn-Artin: A_K is the unique (up to isomorphism) semisimple finite-dimensional ℂ-algebra with three simple blocks of rank 1, 2, 3. (Note: ℍ ⊗_ℝ ℂ = M_2(ℂ), so the Wedderburn-rank of the ℍ block over ℂ is 2.)

- **Step 2 (Definition of A_BdG-full)**: `A_BdG-full = A_F ⊗ M_2(ℂ) = (ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M_3(ℂ)) ⊗ M_2(ℂ)` per CC 1996 + Connes-Moscovici 1995 §III.4 BdG-doubling (Nambu particle-hole grading factor). Distributing the tensor: `A_BdG-full = M_2(ℂ) ⊕ M_2(ℍ) ⊕ M_6(ℂ)`, with Wedderburn-rank vector (2, 4, 6) and total rank `d_total = 12` (per §W8-5 Axis-A WP table line 1043-1048, verified).

- **Step 3 (Definition of inheritance morphism χ)**: `χ : A_K → M_2(ℂ)` is the algebra homomorphism `(c, h, m) ↦ (the M_2(ℂ)-image of the BdG sub-algebra)` per `inheritance-falsifier-protocol.md` line 5. The kernel `ker(χ) ⊇ M_3(ℂ)` by the S89 §W2-3 derived theorem (registry §VII.AZ.OP-PROJ theorem text, registry line 18656, verbatim from S90 W-3 workshop §"What Holds" line 1539): "For every inheritance morphism χ : A_K → T from the substrate algebra `A_K = ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M_3(ℂ)` into a target algebra T satisfying scope condition (C1) `max-Wedderburn-rank(T) < 3`, the M_3(ℂ) Peter-Weyl block is **annihilated**: `χ|_{M_3(ℂ)} = 0`."

- **Step 4 (Substitution: M_3(ℂ) Wedderburn block of A_K is in ker(χ))**: Apply the §VII.AZ.OP-PROJ theorem with T = M_2(ℂ) (max-Wedderburn-rank = 2 < 3, condition C1 satisfied): `χ|_{M_3(ℂ)} = 0`. The kernel-summand structure `ker(χ|_{M_3(ℂ)}) = M_3(ℂ)` is a substrate-IS structural identity at the Wedderburn-Artin + Schur orthogonality axiom layer. Faithfulness of χ requires `ker(χ) = {0}`; but `ker(χ) ⊇ M_3(ℂ) ≠ {0}`. **χ is NOT faithful**.

- **Step 5 (Direction)**: Since χ is not faithful and `ker(χ) ⊇ M_3(ℂ)`, the W6 image-projection (which evaluates Var_a on the M_2(ℂ)-image side ONLY, with the substrate's M_3(ℂ) summand contribution LOST in the kernel) STRUCTURALLY DISCARDS information that IS substrate-IS at the Pillar 1 NCG-axiomatic finite algebra layer. **The W6 reading therefore CANNOT be the substrate-IS canonical reading at Pillar 1**; it can only be substrate-IS at a STRICTLY DOWNSTREAM pillar (Pillar 2 operational laboratory) where the kernel content is irrelevant by construction.

**Why M_3(ℂ) is substrate-IS at Pillar 1 NCG-axiomatic** (Chamseddine-Connes 1996 derivation):

The triple `(A_F, H_F, D_F)` with `A_F = ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M_3(ℂ)` is the UNIQUE (up to KO-dimension-6 isomorphism + axiom 5 grading) finite spectral triple reproducing the Standard Model gauge group `U(1) × SU(2) × SU(3)` via the unitary group of A_F modulo its center, with KO-dim = 6 + 4 = 10 mod 8 = 2 for the product `M^4 × F`. The M_3(ℂ) summand encodes SU(3)-color — the QCD gauge group, the strong force. Removing it via the inheritance morphism χ collapses SU(3)-color to triviality (`χ(M_3(ℂ)) = 0`), which is operationally appropriate at the BdG-restricted 3He-B laboratory pillar (3He-B carries no color charge), but is **structurally incompatible** with the substrate-IS axiomatic identity of A_F at Pillar 1 (Chamseddine-Connes 1996 axiomatic content; agent memory `permanent-theorems.md` lists Chamseddine-Connes derivation as machine-epsilon-verified permanent theorem).

**The (Δ_B/Δ_A)^p cancellation theorem is SILENT on multiplicity content** (S86 W-5 DONE-5):

The cocycle preservation identity `‖[φ_67]‖/‖[φ_88]‖ = 7.324992` (S86 W-5 DONE-5 machine-precision verification at 0.0e+00 residual; canonical_constants.py:`substrate_cocycle_ratio_67_88`) holds because BOTH cocycles φ_67 and φ_88 live UPSTREAM in the M_3(ℂ) Wedderburn block of A_K (per `inheritance-falsifier-protocol.md` line 19 + §VII.AY.OP-PROJ Element 5 registry line 18802). The cancellation theorem operates AT the HH^1 cohomology-class layer (degree-1 Hochschild cocycles on the M_3(ℂ) summand), under common-exponent inheritance. It is **silent** on the MULTIPLICITY question because cocycle ratios `‖[φ_a]‖/‖[φ_b]‖` are dimensionless quantities at the cohomology-class layer, evaluated without reference to multiplicity counts on the spectral decomposition.

Var_a, by contrast, is a TRACE-WEIGHTED MULTIPLICITY-COUNT FUNCTIONAL: it integrates the squared Bogoliubov amplitude `|v_a|^4` against the multiplicity measure `m_a / N` over the substrate's full Peter-Weyl decomposition. The substrate's M_3(ℂ) Wedderburn block contributes 9 = 3² internal degrees of freedom PER SU(3) Peter-Weyl sector at Pillar 1 NCG-axiomatic (per Wedderburn-Artin: `dim_ℂ M_3(ℂ) = 9`), which after BdG-doubling becomes `9 × 2 = 18` in `M_6(ℂ)` per CC 1996 + CM 1995 §III.4 Nambu particle-hole grading. The W5 full-Wedderburn convention preserves this 18-fold multiplicity weighting per substrate eigenvalue (effective `d = 6` for the M_6(ℂ) block per WP line 1047). The W6 image-projection convention discards it (M_3(ℂ) → 0 under χ; only the `M_2(ℂ)` summand with `m=2` per triality-0 sector survives per WP line 1142).

**Volovik's W-5 protocol uses χ at the operational laboratory pillar, NOT the substrate axiom pillar**:

The W-5 calibration corpus (`inheritance-falsifier-protocol.md §"Calibration corpus (W-5)"` lines 84-87) is explicitly designed for **laboratory falsifier protocols** — 3He-B vortex-core spectroscopy at Lancaster MCT-3 / Helsinki ROTA cells; 3He-A µSR. The inheritance morphism χ : A_K → M_2(ℂ) maps "the substrate's parent symmetry IF tested at the BdG-restricted laboratory child realization" — it is a tool for **predicting what the laboratory measures**, NOT a claim about what the substrate IS at the NCG-axiomatic axiom layer. The substrate IS the FULL `(A_K, H_K, D_K)` triple by NCG axioms 1-7 (with A_K = ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M_3(ℂ) per CC 1996); the inheritance morphism χ is a downstream PROJECTION onto a partner-pillar laboratory observable.

**§VII.U.2 T2.46 sub-corrigendum confirms this pillar structure** (registry lines 13028-13049):

The dual-symbol convention adopted at S91 W0 housekeeping per T2.46 sub-corrigendum (registry line 13032) explicitly distinguishes:

- "(a) Pillar 1 NCG-axiomatic canonical: `A_BdG-full = A_F ⊗ M_2(ℂ)` — the substrate-IS observable at the NCG-axiomatic finite-spectral-triple layer per CM-1995 §III.4 finite-spectral-triple residue formula on the FULL substrate algebra A_F = ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M_3(ℂ) tensored with the BdG charge-conjugation block M_2(ℂ)";
- "(b) Pillar 2 operational laboratory canonical: `A_BdG-image = M_2(ℂ)` — the substrate-IS observable at the operational-laboratory pillar via the inheritance morphism χ : A_K = ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M_3(ℂ) → M_2(ℂ)".

The cross-pillar bridge map IS the composition `A_K ↪ A_BdG-full ↠ A_BdG-image` (registry line 13036). My reading: the embedding `A_K ↪ A_BdG-full` is INJECTIVE (faithful at the axiom layer); the projection `A_BdG-full ↠ A_BdG-image` is the inheritance-morphism χ which is **NOT INJECTIVE** (M_3(ℂ) → 0 kernel quotient per registry line 13036). The two halves of the composition are STRUCTURALLY DISTINCT: faithful embedding at Pillar 1 + non-faithful projection at Pillar 2. The W6 image-projection reading is faithful at Pillar 2 (the M_2(ℂ) image side is the natural laboratory algebra for BdG-restricted 3He-B); but it discards substrate content that IS at Pillar 1.

### C3: v_inf = 6.46e-06 reflects a THIRD canonical convention (W5b-47 Corner-II Casimir-bound extrapolation)

**Key finding** (NCG-axiomatic, W5 FULL-WEDDERBURN CANONICAL): The registry-pinned `v_inf_extrapolated = 6.4631783294e-06` (§VII.U.2 Corner II row line 12961; W5b-47 INFO composite, audit_sha256 head `89090d37b3610590…`) is the **asymptotic-L extrapolation of the W5 full-Wedderburn raw L_max=10 anchor 7.282490e-06 via a Weyl-law tail fit `α_loglog = 3.5616` on the substrate-distance-2 Mellin pole `s=4`** per `cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Per-Bulletin-per-pole Level-1 wall classification"`. **Substitution chain** (per `math-scripts.md §"Double-Check Logic Before Compute"`):

- **Step 1 (Definition of SU(3) quadratic Casimir on M_3(ℂ) Peter-Weyl block)**: The SU(3) quadratic Casimir operator `C_2 = Σ_a T_a T_a` (a = 1, ..., 8 Lie-algebra generators) acts on Peter-Weyl SU(3) irrep sectors V_{(p,q)} with eigenvalue `C_2(p,q) = (1/3)(p² + q² + pq + 3p + 3q)` per standard SU(3) representation theory. For the fundamental representation V_{(1,0)} of M_3(ℂ), C_2(1,0) = 4/3; for the adjoint V_{(1,1)}, C_2(1,1) = 3 (the Hochschild cocycles [φ_67] chiral pair + [φ_88] Cartan hypercharge live as degree-1 elements on this adjoint representation). The Casimir operator IS the substrate-IS structural invariant at Pillar 1 that distinguishes SU(3)-color Peter-Weyl sectors; the eigenvalue spectrum `C_2(p,q)` indexes the substrate-distance pole structure per the Mellin-cone closure at substrate-distance pole `s = 2 + C_2(p,q)/M_KK²` (approximate; full structure at `math-scripts.md §"D_K Block-Diagonality Pre-Check"` W11-2 + W11-3 calibration corpus).

- **Step 3 (Substitution: SU(3) Casimir action on Pillar-2 BdG sub-algebra)**: The SU(3) Lie-algebra generators T_a (a = 1, ..., 8) acting on Peter-Weyl SU(3) irrep sectors at Pillar 1 are STRUCTURALLY ABSENT from the Pillar-2 BDI BdG sub-algebra — there are no internal-color quantum numbers in the BDI BdG-restricted single-particle Hilbert space per Step 2.
